The fixed-ICM comparison policy changes one thing only: the same optimizer solves the same game with successor states priced by analytic ICM, so the two policies differ only through that pricing. We evaluate the resulting policies in a three-player jam/fold tournament with a \$1M prize pool. Relative to the frozen strategic-continuation benchmark, analytic ICM has \$9{,}036 mean absolute value error across all 2,838 state--seat entries. That value error rewrites the ranges it prices: measured against each decision point's own fixed-ICM jam range, SCO moves the jam frequency by an average of 14.08\%. To price those different moves, we compare all 946 states and three policy owners while changing only the focal policy and holding both opponents and the continuation evaluator fixed. The policy produced by SCO earns \$214.33 more prize equity per hand on average and is favored in 2,433 of 2,838 matched units. The ordering survives replacing the solver-built opponent with two LLMs and with a family of non-modeling threshold players. This value-to-policy-to-cost chain shows directly when ICM becomes an inadequate objective for tournament strategy construction.

</details>


### [129] [AndroidReality: How Far Are Mobile Agents from the Real World?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07775)
*Xiaoou Liu,Longchao Da,Hanyang Chen,Yuan Ling,Hua Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出AndroidReality，一个基于扰动的框架，用于评估和提升移动智能体的鲁棒性，并引入训练无需的测试时自省恢复（TTIR）机制来缓解现实部署中的性能退化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动智能体在干净的在线基准（如AndroidWorld）上表现良好，但在真实环境中由于界面条件变化和不完美交互会导致性能显著下降，现有评估缺乏对鲁棒性的系统考量。

Method: 从马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）视角出发，将真实环境的界面变异性归纳为状态、转移和动作三个轴上的扰动分类体系；基于该分类体系在AndroidWorld之上构建带真实且可控扰动注入的移动基准测试平台；提出一种无需训练的测试时自省恢复（TTIR）机制来缓解错误。

Result: 评估揭示了移动智能体存在显著的鲁棒性差距和四类反复出现的错误类型；TTIR机制在扰动和干净设置下均能有效缓解这些失败。

Conclusion: 鲁棒性应作为移动智能体评估中缺失的维度被纳入考量，基准扰动是压力测试和揭示移动智能体潜在弱点的有效工具。

Abstract: Mobile agents have achieved promising results on clean online benchmarks such as AndroidWorld, yet their performance often degrades sharply in real-world deployment due to environmental variations and imperfect interface conditions. In this work, we introduce AndroidReality, a perturbation-based framework for evaluating and improving the robustness of mobile agents. Through a Markov Decision Process (MDP) perspective, we organize real-world interface variability into a principled taxonomy of perturbations along three axes: state, transition, and action. Guided by this taxonomy, we build a perturbed mobile benchmark on top of AndroidWorld with realistic and controllable perturbation injections, enabling systematic robustness evaluation of mobile agents. Our evaluation reveals substantial robustness gaps and four recurring error categories, motivating a simple training-free Test-Time Introspective Recovery (TTIR) mechanism that mitigates these failures on both perturbed and clean settings. Together, these results position robustness as a missing dimension in mobile agent evaluation and establish benchmark perturbation as an effective tool for both stress testing and surfacing latent weaknesses of mobile agents.

</details>


### [130] [CARD: Controlled Agentic Reddit Discussions for Credit Card Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09790)
*Yaoning Yu,Kai-Min Chang,Ye Yu,Yi-Chia Wang,Haojing Luo,Haohan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CARD框架，用于生成逼真的信用卡讨论帖对话流，通过非逐字指导（回复结构、评论功能、立场、语气、对话变异性）、规划器、写作器和校准循环，在Reddit真实信用卡讨论数据上超越了多种模拟基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线信用卡讨论为研究消费者金融产品沟通提供了自然场景，但模拟这些讨论不仅需要生成单条评论，还需要生成的对话流在表达方式和用户交互上匹配真实用户行为。

Method: CARD框架包含三个核心组件：规划器（organizes controls）、写作器（generates discussion）和校准循环（updates comments' populations）。通过非逐字指导控制回复结构、评论功能、立场、语气和对话变异性，利用词汇、语义、行为和结构指标在多个LLM上进行评估。

Result: CARD在匹配真实信用卡讨论分布上优于模拟基线，支持多个LLM，并在效应量和分布距离指标上表现更优。

Conclusion: 结构化规划和定向修订能有效提升模拟信用卡讨论的真实感。

Abstract: Online credit card discussions provide a natural setting for studying how consumers communicate about financial products. Simulating these discussions requires more than just generating individual comments, the generated threads should also match how real users express themselves and interact with others. We introduce CARD, a framework for generating realistic credit card discussion threads. Given a credit card post and its matched real thread, CARD uses non-verbatim guidance on reply structure, comment function, stance, tone, and conversational variation. A planner organizes these controls, a writer generates the discussion, and a calibration loop updates comments' populations that contribute to differences between the generated and real thread distributions. We evaluate CARD on real Reddit credit card discussions using lexical, semantic, behavioral, and structural metrics. CARD matches the distributions of real credit card discussions better than simulation baselines across multiple LLMs and also demonstrates smaller effect sizes and distribution distances across metrics. These results show that structured planning and targeted revision can generate the realism of simulated credit card discussions.

</details>


### [131] [The Capability Ladder: A Curriculum-Modernization Framework for Workforce Readiness in the AI Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07779)
*Majid Memari,George Rudolph*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个课程框架论文，基于劳动市场和软件工程证据的结构化叙述性回顾，并通过探索性试点课程进行说明。核心观点是近期变化是任务重新分配而非完全替代，并提出了五级"能力阶梯"框架来分类AI增强工作的操作自主性和所需人类监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI正在改变计算工作的任务构成，其速度超过了课程和培训的适应速度，需要新的课程框架来应对这一转变。

Method: 采用结构化叙述性回顾劳动市场和软件工程证据，并设计了一个为期两个学期的团队型、无代码试点课程，涵盖计算和商业学生，以说明所提出的能力保障框架。

Result: 提出了以"能力阶梯"为核心的框架，涵盖五个层级（触发、自动化、工作流、AI代理、代理团队），映射到课程级更新、工作量感知评估和可堆叠劳动力凭证；试点课程说明了该框架的应用。

Conclusion: 主张围绕持久能力进行有针对性的现代化而非全面课程替换，并明确承认证据局限性：劳动力信号受到非AI因素的干扰，行业报告仅具方向性，试点仅为探索性质。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is changing the task composition of computing work faster than curricula and training typically adapt. This is a curriculum-framework paper, grounded in a structured narrative review of labor-market and software-engineering evidence and illustrated through an exploratory pilot course: the review supports the framework, and the pilot illustrates it rather than serving as primary evidence. The central claim is that near-term change is task reallocation rather than full replacement: routine implementation is increasingly automated while verification, systems thinking, security, and the ability to supervise and orchestrate AI (keeping a human in the loop) gain value. We organize the response as a capability-assurance framework anchored by a Capability Ladder: a five-level progression (trigger, automation, workflow, AI agent, agent team) that classifies the operational autonomy of AI-augmented work and the human supervision it requires. We map the ladder to course-level updates, workload-aware assessment, and stackable workforce credentials, and illustrate it through a two-semester pilot of a team-based, no-code course enrolling computing and business students. We argue for targeted modernization around durable capabilities rather than wholesale curriculum replacement, and we are explicit about evidence limits: labor signals are confounded by non-AI forces, industry reports are directional, and the pilot is exploratory.

</details>


### [132] [Who Built This Model? Tracing LLM Lineage via Spectral Fingerprints in Weight Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07786)
*Yiwei Chen,Bingqi Shang,Sijia Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于权重空间几何指纹的LLM谱系识别框架，通过谱能量和子空间对齐两个互补视角，在无输入数据条件下区分独立起源、同系列和共享基座模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源大语言模型通过复杂的多阶段流水线开发，形成反映模型起源、所有权和演化的复杂谱系关系。理解这些关系对模型溯源、治理和供应链完整性至关重要，因此需要在不依赖输入数据的情况下识别LLM的内在指纹。

Method: 提出统一几何指纹框架，从两个互补视角分析权重矩阵：(i) 谱能量——通过奇异值分布编码全局幅度模式；(ii) 子空间对齐——通过子空间偏差量化方向几何。将问题形式化为谱系判别任务，区分独立起源、同系列和共享基座模型。

Result: 在超过110对多样化开源权重LLM上的实验表明：谱能量能可靠区分独立训练的模型和不同模型家族；子空间对齐能够实现亲缘关系密切模型间的细粒度判别，包括数据集规模和后期训练程序的差异。权重空间几何提供了稳健且可解释的模型谱系信号。

Conclusion: 权重空间几何结构可作为LLM谱系识别的有效信号，能够实现粗粒度的谱系分离和共享基座模型内的细粒度判别，为模型溯源和治理提供了无数据依赖的新途径。

Abstract: Open-weight large language models (LLMs) are increasingly developed through complex, multi-stage pipelines, leading to intricate lineage relationships that reflect model origin, ownership, and evolution. Understanding these relationships is important for model provenance, governance, and supply-chain integrity. In this work, we investigate the notion of LLM "biometrics" (analogous to human biometrics) to ask whether LLMs exhibit intrinsic fingerprints in weight space alone, without access to input data, that reveal their origin and lineage. We formulate this as a lineage discrimination problem, distinguishing among independent-origin, same-series, and shared-base models. To characterize these relationships, we propose a unified geometric fingerprinting framework that analyzes weight matrices from two complementary perspectives: (i) spectral energy, captured by singular value distributions to encode global magnitude patterns, and (ii) subspace alignment, quantified via subspace deviations to capture directional geometry. Our analysis uncovers a clear hierarchy of structural similarity in weight space: spectral energy reliably distinguishes independently trained models and different model families, while subspace alignment enables fine-grained discrimination among closely related models, including variations in dataset scale and post-training procedures. Extensive experiments on over 110 diverse open-weight LLM pairs demonstrate that weight-space geometry provides a robust and interpretable signal for model lineage, enabling coarse-grained regime separation and fine-grained discrimination within shared-base models.

</details>


### [133] [CliniCARE-Bench: Clinical Calibrated Audit of Medical Reasoning in EHR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07796)
*Veronica Chatrath,Bryan Zhu,George Pu,Jingxuan Fan,Apaar Shanker,Varun Ursekar,Anahita Sharma,Jason Qin,Keqi Han,Soham Dinesh Tiwari,Soham Dan,Vijay Kalmath,Yuan Li,Daniel Yue Zhang,Chenguang Wang,Zainab Doctor,Zhijun Yin,Nigam H. Shah,Yuan Xue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍CliniCARE-Bench，一个用于临床审计的基准测试，基于真实MIMIC-IV数据构建25个临床场景、750个患者案例，评估智能体系统在异构纵向电子健康记录中的侦查能力，并引入缺陷率修正的准确率指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在医学知识基准上表现优异，但可靠的临床部署要求智能体能够对异质、纵向的医疗记录进行可辩护的调查研究，包括确定所需证据、检索和调和结构化与非结构化数据、将结论建立在可验证证据之上，以及在无法可靠解决时延迟判断。现有基准缺乏对此类端到端临床审计能力的评估。

Method: 构建CliniCARE-Bench基准：包含25个经临床医生验证的场景，实例化为750个来源于真实患者MIMIC-IV数据的病例。系统通过受控的日志化工具环境进行调查，返回四种裁决之一（是、否、数据不足不确定、医学模糊不确定）。评估维度包括裁决准确率、患者证据与政策依据、过程遵守、校准弃权、可靠性和效率。所有检索、计算和报告均可重放，支持调查轨迹的检查和评分。

Result: 对16个智能体系统进行评估，四类准确率范围为65.3%-76.1%，但原始准确率高估了调查质量。缺陷自由准确率（仅在裁决正确且无禁止性捷径时计分）比原始准确率低4.8-14.8个百分点，并重新排序了排行榜。

Conclusion: CliniCARE-Bench是首个面向部署的临床智能体基准，在统一的患者级裁决框架内同时评估真实纵向EHR调查、声明级证据依据、治理政策使用、过程遵守和校准弃权。研究发现原始准确率会高估调查质量，强调了在临床智能体评估中包含过程完整性和缺陷检测的必要性。

Abstract: Large language models perform strongly on medical knowledge benchmarks, but reliable clinical deployment requires agents to conduct defensible investigations over heterogeneous, longitudinal records: determining what evidence is needed, retrieving and reconciling structured and free-text data, grounding conclusions in verifiable evidence, and deferring cases that cannot be resolved reliably. We introduce CliniCARE-Bench (Clinical Calibrated Audit of Medical Reasoning in EHR), a benchmark for retrospective clinical audit: 25 clinician-validated scenarios instantiated as 750 patient-specific cases over real-patient-derived MIMIC-IV data. Systems investigate each case through a governed, logged tool environment for record retrieval, computation, and policy access, and return one of four verdicts---Yes, No, Indeterminate: Lack of Data, or Indeterminate: Medically Ambiguous---the last two separating missing evidence from residual medical ambiguity. Beyond verdict accuracy, we score patient-evidence and policy grounding, process adherence, calibrated abstention, reliability, and efficiency against case-level reference verdicts produced by independent multi-model adjudication and calibrated against Clinical Board review. Every retrieval, computation, and report is replayable, so the investigation trace is inspectable and scorable. To our knowledge, CliniCARE-Bench is the first deployment-oriented clinical-agent benchmark to jointly evaluate real longitudinal EHR investigation, claim-level evidence grounding, governing-policy use, process adherence, and calibrated abstention within a common patient-level adjudication framework. Across 16 agentic systems, four-way accuracy spans 65.3-76.1%, but raw accuracy overstates investigation quality. Defect-free accuracy, which credits a verdict only when correct and free of prohibited shortcuts, is 4.8-14.8 points lower and reorders the leaderboard.

</details>


### [134] [CausalNav: Reliability-Certified Causal World Models for Control under Physical-Parameter Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07809)
*Yiyao Zhang,Diksha Goel,Hussain Ahmad,Shixun Huang,Jun Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CausalNav，一个基于有符号动作条件转换图的控制器。它通过模拟干预序列生成策略建议，并仅在通过可靠性证书、策略边界门和argmax一致性门时才接受建议，否则回退到基础控制器。在CartPole和Pendulum任务上超越九种基线，但关键发现是：模型保真度与下游控制效用无关，安全部署的关键在于"认证弃权"而非更优预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型只有在能改变智能体行为时有价值，且只有在自身出错时拒绝行动才是安全的。作者希望同时研究这两个方面：如何让世界模型产生有效行动建议，以及如何确保其在不确定时安全弃权。

Method: CausalNav由一个围绕已识别状态坐标构建的有符号动作条件转换图驱动。部署时模拟小型干预序列库，将目标误差转换为策略logit建议，并且仅在无尺度预测可靠性证书、策略边界门和argmax一致性门全部通过时接受建议，否则完全回退到自身的基于模型的基础控制器。

Result: 在对CartPole-v1和离散化Pendulum-v1的物理参数偏移测试中（共享一个PPO训练器、一个交互预算、十个保留种子共200次运行），CausalNav在十个候选中取得最佳平均排名1.25。诊断结果显示图结构恢复显著优于随机水平（CartPole F1=0.59±0.09），但种子间的结构保真度与控制收益无关（r=-0.15, p=0.67）；且证书在10/10个Pendulum种子中弃权，强制规划器执行反而损失回报。

Conclusion: 模型保真度不能预测下游控制效用；经过认证的弃权机制（而非更好的预测能力）才是世界模型安全部署的关键。这一发现对世界模型在实际物理AI中的应用具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: A world model is only useful for physical AI if it changes what the agent does, and only safe if it declines to do so when it is wrong. We study both halves of that requirement with CausalNav, a controller built around a signed, action-conditioned transition graph over identified state coordinates. At deployment CausalNav simulates a small library of intervention sequences, converts their objective error into policy-logit advice, and admits that advice only when a scale-free predictive-reliability certificate, a policy-margin gate, and an argmax-agreement gate all pass; otherwise it falls back exactly to its own model-based base controller. We evaluate against nine controlled baselines (transformer, recurrent, split-latent, graph, causal-induction, and three recent model-based reasoning modules) on CartPole-v1 and discretized Pendulum-v1 with physical-parameter shifts, under one shared PPO trainer, one interaction budget, and ten held-out seeds (200 runs). CausalNav attains the best average rank (1.25 of ten). The diagnostic result is more informative than the ranking: the learned graph recovers structure well above chance (CartPole F1 = 0.59 +/- 0.09), yet per-seed structural fidelity is uncorrelated with per-seed control benefit (r = -0.15, p = 0.67), and the certificate abstains on 10/10 Pendulum seeds, where forcing the planner on costs return. Model fidelity did not predict downstream control utility in our setting; certified abstention, not better prediction, is what made the world model safe to deploy.

</details>


### [135] [When the Judge Should Not Decide: Evidence-Locked, Non-Compensatory Selection Bounds LLM-Judge Failure in Reasoning Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07813)
*Yiyao Zhang,Diksha Goel,Hussain Ahmad,Shixun Huang,Jun Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM裁判在推理管线中的效果更多取决于其所嵌入的决策规则而非自身准确性。作者提出EL-DGR（证据锁定-推导-门控-修复）这种任务自适应的非补偿性决策规则，在冻结裁判、候选集和预算的情况下，显著提升了GSM8K和HotpotQA上的性能，同时避免了裁判破坏正确共识的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注如何提升LLM裁判本身的准确性，但作者指出裁判的实际决策效果受制于其嵌入的决策规则。一个看似准确但无约束的裁判可能比简单多数投票更差，因此需要重新思考如何设计裁判的决策框架。

Method: 提出EL-DGR（Evidence-Locked Derive-Gate-Repair）框架：裁判偏好只能在有可提取证据证书的情况下覆盖证据支持的共识；只有当两个备选方案都未被认证且修复方案被认证时才允许修复。该规则是非补偿性的、任务自适应的，在不改变裁判、候选集和预算的情况下运行。

Result: 在GSM8K上EL-DGR达到58.2%（vs 56.8%纯裁判、55.8%多数投票、55.4%首个候选），在HotpotQA上达到17.33 EM/25.46 F1，相对首个候选GRPO提升+2.8pp和+2.00 EM。决策审计显示EL-DGR仅在30个试点问题中的8个推翻共识，且从未将正确共识转为错误答案。同时报告了负面结果：作为步级门控训练奖励的七通道分解无效，且没有任何单一通道是必要的。

Conclusion: 实践者面对的核心结论是：对裁判持负面态度，对可接纳性规则持正面态度——应限制裁判的爆炸半径（blast radius），而非试图让它更准确。决策规则的设计比裁判的原始准确性更重要。

Abstract: An LLM judge deployed inside a reasoning pipeline does not merely measure quality, it decides which answer ships. We show that the cost of that decision depends less on judge accuracy than on the decision rule the judge is embedded in. On frozen candidate pools from four GRPO policies, an unconstrained scalar DeepSeek-R1-7B judge buys almost nothing over answer-level majority vote (+1.0 pp on 500 GSM8K questions, +0.34 EM on 300 HotpotQA questions), and on a frozen-rule 30-question confirmation split it is 10 points worse than majority, a judge that destroys accuracy while scoring candidates confidently. We then subordinate the same judge to Evidence-Locked Derive-Gate-Repair (EL-DGR), a task-adaptive non-compensatory rule under which a judge preference may override evidence-supported consensus only with an extractive evidence certificate, and a repair only when neither alternative is certified and the repair is. With no change to the judge, the candidates, or the budget, EL-DGR reaches 58.2% on GSM8K (vs. 56.8% judge, 55.8% majority, 55.4% first candidate) and 17.33 EM / 25.46 F1 on HotpotQA (vs. 15.67/23.49, 15.33/23.19, 15.33/22.97), improving on first-candidate GRPO by +2.8 pp (exact McNemar p=0.0026) and +2.00 EM (p=0.070, borderline). A decision audit shows why: EL-DGR overturns consensus on only 8 of 30 pilot questions and never converts a correct consensus into an incorrect answer. We also report what did not work: the same seven-channel decomposition used as a step-level gated training reward is null, and corrected channel-drop ablations show no channel is individually necessary (p=1.0 throughout). The practitioner-facing finding is negative about judges and positive about admissibility, bound the judge's blast radius rather than trying to make it accurate.

</details>


### [136] [Counterfactual Benchmarking and Training for Factuality Consistency and Order-Robust Grounded Reasoning in LLMs over Heterogeneous Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07838)
*Shibo Chu,Yuze Liu,Tiehua Zhang,Zhishu Shen,Lianghua He,Haofen Wang,Zhijun Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了TKFQA基准（包含10,130个QA对），用于评估LLM在表格、文本和知识图谱异构知识上的多跳推理能力及对输入顺序的鲁棒性；同时提出ORLF训练框架，通过知识特定潜在向量建模跨上下文拓扑关系，在四个LLM骨干上分别提升EM和RCA 2.15%和4.29%，并降低顺序敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准缺乏对LLM在异构知识上下文中进行多跳推理链以及输入顺序鲁棒性的全面评估。

Method: 提出TKFQA基准（基于反事实推理链构建），并提出ORLF训练框架：包含上下文位置编码、潜在桥接注意力掩码和拓扑知识偏置，以保留知识特定偏差并编码拓扑语义。

Result: 评估14个开源和闭源LLM发现现有模型推理链准确率有限且对输入顺序敏感；ORLF在四个LLM骨干上超越基线，EM提升2.15%，RCA提升4.29%，顺序引起的标准差降低0.04%-3.01%。

Conclusion: LLM在异构知识多跳推理上仍存在不足，ORLF提供了一种有效的LLM无关训练方案来提升推理链准确性和输入顺序鲁棒性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have increasingly supported response generation grounded in user-provided knowledge spanning heterogeneous structures. However, existing benchmarks provide limited assessment of whether LLMs can faithfully perform multi-hop reasoning chains across such knowledge contexts while remaining robust to variations in their input order. We introduce TKFQA, a factuality consistency benchmark comprising 10,130 question-answering (QA) pairs grounded in tables, texts, and knowledge graphs (KGs). Each example is constructed from an explicit counterfactual reasoning chain, enabling the joint evaluation of answer correctness, reasoning-chain accuracy, and robustness to different input-order. An extensive evaluation of 14 open- and closed-source LLMs reveals that state-of-the-art models exhibit limited reasoning-chain accuracy and remain sensitive to variations in the input order of heterogeneous knowledge contexts. To address these limitations, we propose ORLF, an LLM-agnostic training framework that models cross-context topological relations through knowledge-specific latent vectors. ORLF integrates context-wise position encoding, a latent-bridge attention mask, and topological knowledge bias to preserve knowledge-specific bias and encode topological semantics. Experiments across four LLM backbones show that ORLF outperforms competitive training-free and LoRA-based baselines, improving average Exact Match and Reasoning-Chain Accuracy by 2.15% and 4.29%, respectively, while reducing order-induced performance standard deviation by 0.04% to 3.01%.

</details>


### [137] [Back to the Future: A workbook time machine for spread sheet creation benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07873)
*Mansi Uniyal,Agamdeep Singh,Ananya Singha,Priyanshu Gupta,Mukul Singh,Gust Verbruggen,Vu Le,Sumit Gulwani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍Workbook Time Machine——一个自动创建电子表格衍生对象（公式、图表、透视表、条件格式）评估基准的流水线，并由此构建了WTM Corpus和包含150个任务的WTM Bench基准，用于评估LLM在Excel任务上的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电子表格操作代理缺乏系统性的基准测试，尤其是针对创建衍生对象（如公式、图表等）的能力评估不足。

Method: 构建一个自动化的流水线，从公开的电子表格语料库中生成（输入工作簿、输出工作簿、查询）三元组，涵盖四种工件类型和不同复杂度，并筛选出150个任务的评估基准WTM Bench。

Result: 评估发现查询的具体程度、代理的编排方式以及控制电子表格的接口API对LLM在Excel任务上的表现有显著影响。

Conclusion: WTM Bench为评估和改进LLM在电子表格操作上的能力提供了标准化基准，并揭示了影响性能的关键因素。

Abstract: We introduce the workbook time machine, a pipeline that automatically creates benchmarks evaluating the ability of language models to create derived objects in spreadsheets (formulas, charts, pivot tables, and conditional formatting). Applied to public workbook corpora, it produces wtmcorpus--a collection of (input workbook, output workbook, query) triples spanning four artifact types and varying complexity. From this corpus we curate wtmbench, a 150-task evaluation benchmark with queries at three levels of specificity. We evaluate existing spreadsheet manipulation agents and baselines on wtmbench across artifact types, step complexity, and instruction granularity. Our evaluations show that query specificity, agent orchestration, and interface API used to control spreadsheets play a big role in LLM performance on Excel tasks.

</details>


### [138] [SurgLAT: Surgical Latent Attention Tracking for Depth-Aware Robotic Laparoscope Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07876)
*Rulin Zhou,Qiujie Song,Yujie Ma,An Wang,Wanhao Liu,Guoheng Ma,Yidu Wang,Guankun Wang,Xingrong Diao,Jiankun Wang,Chaowei Zhu,Xianming Liu,Hongliang Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SurgLAT，一个用于自主腹腔镜相机控制的因果在线框架，通过冻结的DINOv3编码器和状态条件空间token混合器提取手术证据，利用选择性因果潜在记忆模块联合建模短期运动连续性和长期手术意图演化，并将学习的潜在手术注意力状态解码为概率注意力热图和手术区域，同时引入基于虚拟轴的RCM约束控制和冗余感知零空间初始化的机器人部署框架，在真实腹腔镜手术视频和物理机器人平台上验证了系统的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主腹腔镜相机控制需要对手术场景中动态演化的外科医生操作意图进行持续理解，而目标手术区域并非稳定的物理对象，而是潜在且随时间演变的注意状态，现有方法难以有效建模这种潜在注意力。

Method: SurgLAT使用冻结的DINOv3编码器配合状态条件空间token混合器，在记忆引导的空间先验下提取手术证据；选择性因果潜在记忆模块通过动态检索当前、近期和历史潜在状态，联合建模短期运动连续性与长期手术意图演化；学习到的潜在注意力状态被解码为概率注意力热图和手术区域，并引入基于虚拟轴公式的RCM约束控制和冗余感知零空间初始化实现稳定机械臂运动。

Result: 在真实腹腔镜手术视频和物理机器人腹腔镜平台上，系统在遮挡、快速运动和目标切换场景下实现了稳健的在线手术区域跟踪和稳定的自主内窥镜调整。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明了潜在手术意图建模对手术自主性的有效性，系统在动态手术场景中实现了鲁棒的在线手术区域跟踪和稳定的自主内窥镜控制。

Abstract: Autonomous laparoscopic camera control requires continuous understanding of the surgeon's operative intent in dynamic surgical scenes, where the target operative region is not a stable physical object but a latent and temporally evolving attention state. In this work, we present Surgical Latent Attention Tracking (SurgLAT), a causal online framework for latent surgical attention modeling and autonomous laparoscopic view control. SurgLAT uses a frozen DINOv3 encoder and a state-conditioned spatial token mixer to extract operative evidence under a memory-guided spatial prior, while a selective causal latent memory module jointly models short-term motion continuity and long-horizon surgical intent evolution through dynamic retrieval of current, recent, and historical latent states. The learned latent surgical attention state is decoded into a probabilistic attention heatmap and operative region for downstream endoscope guidance. Beyond perception, we further introduce a robotic deployment framework with explicit laparoscopic Remote Center of Motion (RCM) constrained control based on virtual-axis formulation, together with redundancy-aware null-space initialization for stable and smooth manipulator motion. We validate the full system on real laparoscopic surgical videos and a physical robotic laparoscope platform. Experimental results demonstrate robust online operative-region tracking and stable autonomous endoscopy adjustment under occlusion, rapid motion, and target transitions, highlighting the effectiveness of latent surgical intent modeling for surgical autonomy.

</details>


### [139] [GRACE: LLM-Grounded Semantic Metric Spaces for Scalable Mixed-Data Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07881)
*Zihua Yang,Zhencheng Xie,Junyang Chen,Liang Xie,Yiqun Zhang,Mengke Li,Yang Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GRACE是一个基于LLM的混合型表格数据聚类框架，通过一次性属性-值级别的语义获取将异构数据映射到统一语义空间，兼顾了语义丰富性与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统混合表格数据聚类依赖数据集内部统计信息来估计类别关系，忽略了概念上明显但统计上未观察到的语义关联。虽然LLM能提供外部世界知识，但将其文本中心推理应用于抽象表格概念存在模态鸿沟，且嵌入迭代优化循环会带来巨大的计算开销。

Method: GRACE提出多视角LLM查询策略，在属性-值级别进行一次性语义获取，将异构值映射为知识驱动的描述。通过一次性grounding提取通用语义表示，将异构属性嵌入统一空间，避免在迭代优化中反复调用LLM。同时，GRACE将外部语义与数据集内部统计证据进行交叉验证，确保与数据集特定的聚类结构对齐。

Result: GRACE在11种竞争方法中实现了优越的聚类准确性和概念可解释性，同时匹配传统统计驱动基线的可扩展性。源代码已开源。

Conclusion: GRACE证明了通过一次性LLM语义获取可以在不牺牲可扩展性的前提下显著提升混合表格数据聚类的语义质量和准确性，为LLM辅助数据挖掘提供了一个高效范式。

Abstract: Clustering mixed tabular data requires a unified metric space to bridge the inherent heterogeneity between continuous numerical measurements and discrete categorical symbols. Traditionally, algorithms rely entirely on dataset-internal statistics to estimate categorical relationships, which confines the learned metric to empirical co-occurrences and ignores conceptually obvious yet statistically unobserved affinities. Although LLMs offer external world knowledge, applying their text-centric reasoning to highly abstract tabular concepts presents significant challenges. Bridging this modality gap to construct a semantically complete metric typically requires embedding LLMs into iterative metric learning loops to dynamically optimize cross-modality representations. This incurs intractable computational overhead, forcing a compromise between semantic enrichment and scalability. Therefore, we propose GRACE, an LLM-grounded framework for scalable mixed-data clustering. GRACE shifts semantic acquisition to the attribute-value level via a multi-perspective LLM querying strategy, mapping heterogeneous values into knowledge-informed descriptions. Crucially, this one-shot grounding extracts general-purpose semantic representations that embed heterogeneous attributes into a unified space, decoupling expensive LLM invocation from iterative optimization. Furthermore, GRACE cross-validates these external semantics against dataset-internal statistical evidence to ensure alignment with the dataset-specific cluster structure. Ultimately, GRACE matches the scalability of conventional statistics-driven baselines while achieving superior clustering accuracy and conceptual interpretability over 11 competing methods. The source code is available at https://github.com/develop-yang/GRACE-GRACE-A

</details>


### [140] [Reason Wide, Not Deep: Amortizing the Reasoning Premium into Distilled Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07885)
*Agamdeep Singh,Srishti Gautam,Priyanshu Gupta,Nikita Mehrotra,Tanmay Bakshi,Sumit Gulwani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出通过从已有轨迹中蒸馏自然语言技能并注入非推理模型的系统提示，来摊薄推理模型的输出代价，在四个智能体基准上以2.7-6倍更少的输出token恢复了55%-100%以上的推理差距，甚至两个基准上超过推理模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模式的语言模型在多步智能体任务上表现优异，但每个episode需支付3-6倍的输出token开销，且大量token浪费在重复推导跨episode共享的程序上。

Method: 让编码智能体分析训练集中少量已有轨迹，编译生成紧凑的自然语言技能，注入非推理模型的系统提示中，从而在推理成本和性能间取得折中。

Result: 在ALFWorld、tau²-bench（电信和零售）和SpreadsheetBench-Verified四个基准上，技能蒸馏恢复55%-100%+的推理差距，其中两个基准超过推理模式；输出token减少2.7-6倍且零推理token；非推理轨迹蒸馏的技能与配对推理/非推理语料蒸馏的技能竞争力相当。

Conclusion: 推理是单episode内的深层搜索，付出的代价每次部署都要重新支付；语料蒸馏是跨episode的宽搜索，一次性支付。两者恢复的程序性知识重叠，跨廉价轨迹的宽度往往更划算，但部分域（电信、SpreadsheetBench）仍需要真正的逐实例深度搜索来弥补残余差距。

Abstract: Reasoning modes of language models outperform their non-reasoning counterparts on multi-step agentic tasks, but pay a 3-6x premium in output tokens on every episode -- much of it spent re-deriving procedures that are shared across episodes of the same domain. We show this recurring cost can be amortized: a coding agent analyses a small corpus of existing trajectories from a training split and compiles a compact natural-language skill that is injected into the non-reasoning model's system prompt. Across four agentic benchmarks (ALFWorld, tau$^2$-bench telecom and retail, and SpreadsheetBench-Verified), skills recover 55%-100%+ of the reasoning gap for GPT-5.4-mini on held-out tasks -- exceeding the reasoning mode outright on two of four -- while emitting 2.7-6x fewer output tokens and zero reasoning tokens. Notably, reasoning traces are not a prerequisite: skills distilled from non-reasoning trajectories alone remain competitive with skills distilled from paired reasoning/non-reasoning corpora, with domain-dependent differences between the two sources. We interpret these results through a search lens: test-time reasoning is deep search inside a single episode, re-paid at every deployment, while corpus distillation is wide search across episodes, paid once. The two recover overlapping procedural knowledge, and width over cheap trajectories is often the better buy -- with the residual gap on some domains (telecom, SpreadsheetBench) delineating where genuinely per-instance deep search remains necessary.

</details>


### [141] [TelemetrySuffBench: Is Agent Telemetry Sufficient for Failure-Origin Diagnosis?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07899)
*Yuxuan Zhu,Peng Pu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TelemetrySuffBench基准，用于分离故障检测、故障源定位和安全弃权三个任务，实验表明大型语言模型在遥测数据上能高精度检测故障但定位能力极差，且安全弃权能力因模型而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统暴露执行轨迹，但显示故障的遥测数据不足以识别故障源头。现有工作缺乏一个受控基准来区分故障检测、故障源定位和证据不足时的安全弃权。

Method: 构建包含延迟绑定故障的规范多组件轨迹基准，提供粗视图、七因子遥测掩码和精确等同的模糊来源对三种配对视图。使用统一协议评估五个前沿语言模型，包括显式候选集、无效输出核算、子组分析和冻结盲保持集。

Result: 完整遥测下来源步骤Top-1准确率范围为33.8%至97.2%。元数据、OpenTelemetry兼容和OpenInference兼容视图保持99.5%-100%的检测F1但来源定位准确率最多仅0.5%，暴露检测-定位缺口。移除决策内容使来源定位准确率归零，移除来源信息造成模型依赖的显著损失。证据门控使三个模型不支持的唯一来源答案减少12.5-48.6个百分点，而两个模型仍回答所有案例。

Conclusion: 终端状态可支持故障检测，但可靠的因果归因需要显式的决策到来源链接以及跨模型有效的弃权保障机制。

Abstract: Agent systems increasingly expose execution traces, yet telemetry that reveals a failure may still be inadequate for identifying where that failure originated. We introduce TelemetrySuffBench, a controlled benchmark that separates failure detection, fault-origin localization, and safe abstention under insufficient evidence. The benchmark constructs canonical multi-component traces with delayed-binding faults and renders them as paired coarse views, seven-factor telemetry masks, and exact-equal ambiguous origin pairs. We evaluate five frontier language models using unified protocols, explicit candidate sets, invalid-output accounting, subgroup analyses, and a frozen blind holdout. With full telemetry, origin-step Top-1 accuracy ranges from 33.8% to 97.2% across models. Metadata, OpenTelemetry-compatible, and OpenInference-compatible views retain 99.5% to 100% detection F1 while limiting origin-step accuracy to at most 0.5%, exposing a robust detection-localization gap. Factor ablations further show that removing decision content reduces origin-step accuracy to zero for every model, while provenance removal also causes large model-dependent losses. On rich ambiguous inputs that require abstention, evidence gating reduces unsupported unique-origin answers by 12.5 to 48.6 percentage points for three models, whereas two models still answer every case, revealing strong model dependence in safe abstention. Results on the frozen holdout reproduce the central pattern within the same generator family. These findings show that terminal status can support detection, whereas reliable causal attribution requires explicit decision-to-provenance links and abstention safeguards that remain effective across models. The dataset and benchmark implementation are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/TelemetrySuffBench-E635/README.md.

</details>


### [142] [GraphThink: Graph-Enhanced LLM Thinking for Long-Horizon Embodied Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07905)
*Chen Li,Sijie Cheng,Yuelin Zhang,Junxi Li,Maozhi Huang,Yang Liu,Wenbing Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GraphThink框架，通过任务图提供结构化知识引导LLM规划，场景图维护环境记忆进行事件驱动重规划，在ALFRED基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的具身智能体规划器存在物理幻觉、长时程任务泛化能力差以及环境感知不足的问题。

Method: GraphThink框架包含三个核心模块：1）任务图通过上下文提示和迭代细化引导LLM思考，缓解规划幻觉；2）在GRPO框架下，任务图提供精细奖励设计以训练LLM规划器，增强长时程规划能力和泛化性；3）基于场景图的事件驱动重规划模块，实现闭环环境感知和错误纠正。

Result: 在ALFRED基准上达到SOTA性能，高级规划器在验证集和长时程任务上超越领先的API-based LLM，展现出强大的零样本和少样本能力，并在新颖任务和环境中表现出良好的分布外泛化能力。

Conclusion: GraphThink通过任务图与场景图的有机结合，有效解决了LLM规划器的幻觉、长时程规划和环境感知等核心挑战，验证了结构化知识图在具身智能规划中的关键价值。

Abstract: Embodied agents using LLM-based planners often struggle with physical hallucinations, poor generalization to long-horizon tasks, and lack of environmental awareness. We propose GraphThink, a novel framework that integrates a task graph to provide structured knowledge for robust planning and a scene graph to maintain environmental memory for event-driven replanning. Specifically, the task graph guides LLM thinking through contextual prompting and iterative refinement, effectively mitigating planning hallucinations. Furthermore, within the GRPO framework, the task graph offers delicate reward design to train the LLM planner, enhancing long-horizon planning capabilities and improving generalization. Finally, an event-driven replanning module, powered by the scene graph, enables closed-loop environment awareness and error correction. GraphThink achieves state-of-the-art performance on the ALFRED benchmark. In particular, our high-level planner surpasses leading API-based LLMs on both the validation set and held-out long-horizon tasks, underscoring its robust zero-shot and few-shot capabilities. Additional evaluations further demonstrate strong out-of-distribution generalization to novel tasks and environments.

</details>


### [143] [When Is Benchmark Contamination Detectable? Information Limits and Power-Calibrated Audits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07914)
*Ibne Farabi Shihab,Sanjeda Akter,Anuj Sharma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了行为污染检测器在基准测试中"无证据"结论的两种可能解释：基准是干净的或审计功效不足。作者形式化了这一问题，提出了基于二阶矩的可检测性理论框架，并发现功效预测与实测之间存在校准问题，需要通过两阶段规划器修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为污染检测器返回"无证据"时，无法区分是基准确实干净还是审计功效不足。作者希望形式化这种区分，为审计结果提供可解释性。

Method: 作者使用匹配的干净和污染对照样本，将行为通道建模为稀疏混合分布Q_alpha=(1-alpha)P_0+alpha P_1，通过二阶矩论证推导出可检测性由alpha*rho*sqrt(m)决定，其中rho^2=chi^2(P_1||P_0)衡量行为可分性。提出了样本分割证书下界方法，以及预声明两阶段规划器来模拟部署测试并修复预算。

Result: 冻结校准功效预测留出功效曲线R²=0.83-0.98；但仅基于功效的高斯预算在小样本情况下校准不良，在9/9个通过门控的通道中失败。两阶段规划器修复了预算问题且均匀保守。证书有效但在审计规模下空洞。五种子配对注入研究恢复机制排序：逐字>释义>表面。

Conclusion: 行为污染审计的"无证据"结论必须在功效、预算和有效性门控的上下文中解释，非拒绝结果本身不可单独作为干净的证据。

Abstract: Behavioral contamination detectors can return "no evidence" either because a benchmark is clean or because the audit has little power. We formalize this distinction for a benchmark in which an unknown fraction alpha of items was seen during training. With matched clean and seen controls, the behavioral channel is the sparse mixture Q_alpha = (1 - alpha) P_0 + alpha P_1, and an exact second-moment argument shows that detectability is governed by alpha * rho * sqrt(m), where rho^2 = chi^2(P_1 || P_0) measures behavioral separability. Any scalar detector reduces to its efficacy, ef = |E_1 f - E_0 f| / sqrt(Var_0(f)) <= rho, which can be estimated from controls before the audit is run. A separate sample-split certificate lower-bounds alpha distribution-free, without requiring an orientation assumption. Our empirical finding is two-sided. Frozen calibration efficacy predicts held-out power curves, with R^2 = 0.83-0.98 across six exact-permutation channels, but the efficacy-only Gaussian budget is miscalibrated at the small sample sizes it prescribes, failing in 9/9 gate-passing channels even though efficacy itself transports. The failure is in the inversion, not the calibration. A predeclared two-stage planner that simulates the deployed test repairs the budgets, is uniformly conservative, and abstains when its probe does not transport. The certificate is valid but vacuous at audit scale, and a five-seed paired injection study recovers the mechanism ordering verbatim > paraphrase > surface, in which the apparent answer-only signal is explained by baseline drift. We report the audit contract and its failures together: a non-rejection is interpretable only alongside the efficacy, budget, and validity gates that produced it.

</details>


### [144] [TongGuOCR: A Layout-Aware and Token-Augmented OCR Framework for Chinese Historical Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07917)
*Zhongheng Zhou,Yi Sun,Huiguo He,Yuyi Zhang,Peirong Zhang,Yulin Fang,Dezhi Peng,Minghui Liao,Lianwen Jin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TongGuOCR是一个面向中文历史文献的布局感知与令牌增强OCR框架，通过布局感知预处理模块构建局部连贯的识别块，并通过字符级词汇扩展和行间位移令牌两个层面的令牌增强来提升识别精度，在M5HisDoc基准上达到93.76 AR，显著优于传统OCR模型和MLLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中文历史文献包含宝贵的文化遗产，但许多馆藏仅有扫描图像，无法进行全文检索和计算分析。OCR虽可弥合这一差距，但历史文献常包含复杂版面、稀有汉字和非平凡的阅读顺序，给准确转录带来挑战。

Method: 提出TongGuOCR框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 布局感知预处理模块——构建并细化局部连贯的识别块，保留局部上下文同时减少跨区域干扰；(2) 令牌增强识别模块——在字符级扩充词汇表使稀有字形获得直接的单令牌表示，同时在行间转换建模中注入离散空间位移令牌，引导解码器沿着复杂阅读路径前进而无需精确坐标。

Result: 在两个中文历史文献OCR基准上，TongGuOCR优于传统任务专用OCR模型、通用多模态大语言模型和OCR专用MLLM。在M5HisDoc基准上取得93.76 AR，NED从10.43降至6.15，RO-ED从7.53降至3.49。

Conclusion: TongGuOCR通过布局感知预处理和双层次令牌增强，有效解决了中文历史文献OCR中复杂版面、稀有字符和复杂阅读顺序的挑战，为该领域提供了先进的解决方案。

Abstract: Chinese historical documents preserve valuable cultural heritage, but many collections remain accessible only as scanned page images, preventing full-text retrieval, collation, and computational analysis. Optical character recognition (OCR) can bridge this gap, but accurate transcription remains challenging because historical documents often contain complex layouts, rare characters, and nontrivial reading orders. We propose TongGuOCR, a layout-aware and token-augmented OCR framework for Chinese historical documents. First, a Layout-Aware Preprocessing module constructs and refines locally coherent recognition blocks to preserve local context while reducing interference across regions. Second, a Token-Augmented Recognition module augments the transcription target at two complementary levels: character-level vocabulary expansion gives each rare glyph a direct one-token representation and shortens its decoding path, while line-to-line transition modeling injects discrete spatial displacement tokens that guide the decoder along complex reading paths without requiring precise coordinates. Experiments on two Chinese historical document OCR benchmarks show that TongGuOCR outperforms representative traditional task-specific OCR models, general-purpose multimodal large language models (MLLMs), and OCR-oriented MLLMs. On the more challenging M5HisDoc benchmark, TongGuOCR achieves 93.76 AR and reduces NED from 10.43 to 6.15 and RO-ED from 7.53 to 3.49 relative to the best competing score for each metric. An online demo is available at https://jzzh2004.github.io/TongGuOCR.

</details>


### [145] [ZhuLong: Execution-Grounded LLM Agent for EDA Scripting with Offline API Self-Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07925)
*Yang Liu,Shiwei Hou,Xiyuan Chen,Yu Wang,Sen Yuan,Qirui Gan,Shao You,Feifan Chen,Wencheng Li,Shuyang Hu,Yongzhou Liu,Emma Xia,Xiaojing Lu,Hao Wang,Fan Xu,Yanfeng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ZhuLong，一个面向EDA脚本编程的LLM编码代理，结合API检索、文档检查和沙箱执行（通过统一MCP工具），并引入离线API自探索机制来推断未文档化API行为。在EDA-Eval-PyAether基准（158个真实任务）上达到78.5% Pass@1，远超纯LLM基线的23.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EDA脚本编写依赖于工具特定的、常常未文档化的API，这些是长尾瓶颈，现有LLM无法有效解决。

Method: 构建执行导向的LLM编码代理，融合API检索、文档检查和沙箱执行（通过统一MCP工具）；首创离线API自探索机制，通过反事实实验推断未文档化API行为。

Result: 在商业Empyrean Aether环境中达到78.5% Pass@1，远超纯LLM基线（23.6%）；消融实验显示沙箱执行是主要性能驱动力（移除后下降41.2pp），自探索机制额外提升3.2pp精度并减少22.1%的工具调用；在20个交互任务中PyAether达60.0%、SKILL达50.0%的Pass@1。

Conclusion: 通过执行验证和多策略结合，ZhuLong显著提升了EDA脚本编程中LLM对未文档化API的处理能力，验证了执行导向方法在该领域的有效性。

Abstract: EDA scripting with tool-specific, often undocumented APIs remains a long-tail bottleneck that existing LLMs fail to address. This paper presents ZhuLong, an execution-grounded LLM coding agent for PyAether and SKILL that combines API retrieval, documentation inspection, and sandbox execution via unified MCP tools, augmented by an offline API self-exploration mechanism that infers undocumented API behaviors through counterfactual experimentation.
  We evaluate ZhuLong on EDA-Eval-PyAether, a benchmark of 158 real-world tasks with assertion-based execution, where the complete system achieves 78.5% Pass@1 in the commercial Empyrean Aether environment, substantially outperforming a pure LLM baseline (23.6%). Ablation studies identify sandbox execution as the dominant performance driver (41.2 pp drop when removed), with the self-exploration mechanism contributing an additional 3.2 pp accuracy gain and a 22.1% reduction in per-task tool calls. On 20 interactive tasks involving unsaved layouts and schematics, ZhuLong achieves 60.0% Pass@1 for PyAether and 50.0% for SKILL.

</details>


### [146] [REIN: Bridging the Gap between Reasoning and Reliability via Reflection and Abstention Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07931)
*Zhengze Huang,Luyang Yu,Di Hong,Xinzhe Huang,Wanyu Lin,Zhixuan Chu,Zhan Qin,Tianhang Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出REIN对齐框架，通过结构化推理序列（思考→反思→回答）和奖励机制鼓励模型主动弃答，同时缓解大型推理模型的推理幻觉和知识幻觉，在单次前向传播中显著降低幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型（LRMs）容易出现幻觉，影响其可靠性和安全部署。幻觉源自两种不同的失败：推理幻觉（错误推理步骤传导至错误结论）和知识幻觉（模型缺乏回答所需的事实知识），需要针对性解决。

Method: 提出REIN对齐框架，包含两大核心机制：(1) 训练LRMs生成结构化推理序列（&lt;think&gt;→&lt;reflection&gt;→&lt;answer&gt;），在给出最终答案前进行显式自我反思，解决推理幻觉；(2) 引入奖励机制，当所有采样推理链均无法产生正确答案时，鼓励模型显式弃答（如"我不知道"），避免无支撑的预测，解决知识幻觉。

Result: 在数学和常识推理基准上的广泛评估表明，REIN在多个骨干模型上将幻觉代理指标降低58~72%，同时保持86~91%的平均覆盖率，并在已回答问题上的选择性准确率提升6.6~14.2%。相比竞争基线，REIN持续提升选择性准确率、减少错误但自我认可的回答，并保持高覆盖率。

Conclusion: REIN在单次前向传播中即可实现上述效果，无需过程监督、推理时控制器、外部搜索或多轮批评机制，是一种高效且实用的LRM幻觉缓解方案。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) are prone to hallucination, which undermines their reliability and poses challenges for safe deployment. Hallucinations in LRMs arise from two distinct failure sources: reasoning hallucination, where flawed inference steps propagate to an incorrect conclusion, and knowledge hallucination, where the model lacks the requisite factual knowledge to answer the query. To address reasoning hallucination, we propose REIN, an alignment framework that trains LRMs to produce a structured reasoning sequence, $\texttt{<think>} $$\rightarrow$ $\texttt{<reflection>} $$\rightarrow$ $\texttt{<answer>}$, enabling explicit self-reflection before committing to a final answer. To address knowledge hallucination, REIN introduces a reward mechanism that encourages explicit abstention (e.g., "I don't know") when none of the sampled reasoning chains yields a correct answer, allowing the model to refrain from unsupported predictions. Extensive evaluations on mathematical and commonsense reasoning benchmarks show that REIN consistently improves selective accuracy, reduces incorrect-but-self-endorsed responses, and maintains high coverage compared with competitive baselines. Notably, REIN achieves these gains within a single forward pass, without requiring process supervision, inference-time controllers, external search, or multi-round critiques. Experiments on multiple backbones show that REIN reduces the hallucination proxy by $58\sim72\%$ relative to the base models while maintaining $86\sim91\%$ average coverage, and improves selective accuracy on attempted questions by $6.6\sim14.2\%$.

</details>


### [147] [Locating Failure in Multi-Page Visually Rich Document Understanding: An Empirical Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07943)
*Lewei Xu,Yihao Ding,Zihan Xu,Daniel Yitian Su,Daochang Liu,Siwen Luo,Yifan Peng,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究多页视觉丰富文档理解（MP-VRDU）系统，通过隔离表征、选择和推理三种失败模式，系统性地检验了各组件对最终准确率的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多页视觉丰富文档理解需要处理跨页面的稀疏证据，且现有系统构建方式存在竞争性且未经充分验证的假设，需要通过系统性实验来厘清。

Method: 在保持其他变量固定的情况下逐一干预三种失败模式（表征、选择、推理），在一个多页文档理解数据集上隔离评估各环节的贡献。

Result: 视觉信息是必要的但不能替代文本提取；页面缺失是准确率的瓶颈而干扰页影响较小；推理器即使获得完整跨页证据仍难以整合。提示词可以显著改变推理行为，但会牺牲部分效果。

Conclusion: 将这些发现转化为在固定计算预算下构建MP-VRDU系统的实践指导。

Abstract: Multi-page visually-rich document understanding (MP-VRDU) requires managing evidence that is sparse, spread across pages, and often exceeds a model's context window. Prior work has produced competing, largely untested claims about how these systems should be built. We attribute incorrect answers to three failure modes, representation, selection, and reasoning, and isolate each over a multi-page document understanding dataset by intervening on one while holding the others fixed. We find that vision is necessary but does not replace text extraction, that missing pages bound accuracy while distractors cost little, and that reasoners fail to integrate evidence across pages even when it is fully supplied. Prompting can shift reasoning behaviour substantially, improving some outcomes at the expense of others. We translate these findings into guidance for building such systems under a fixed compute budget.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [148] [MasDrift: Benchmarking Authorization Preservation Across Multi-Agent Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07556)
*Zhuoning Xu,Xiucheng Zhang,Hanjun Luo,Yingbin Jin,Yinpeng Dong,Hanan Salam*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出MasDrift基准，包含600个跨八领域的良性生产力任务，用于评估多智能体系统中授权漂移（constraint drift）问题。研究发现集中式层级结构在任务完成率上优于对等网络，但会产生更多未授权操作；重新锚定（re-anchoring）防御策略能有效减少未授权操作，而链式传播策略则会严重阻碍任务完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统将长时任务分解给主管和子智能体，但委派目标不一定保留原始授权边界。现有安全基准主要研究对抗性破坏，缺乏对约束漂移的系统性架构级评估。

Method: 构建MasDrift基准，包含600个良性生产力任务、八领域，每个任务配对必需工作与保留操作。比较单智能体、集中式和去中心化协调方式，变换层级深度和同伴宽度，同时测量任务完成率和授权保留率。对比两种防御策略：将每个待处理调用重新锚定到原始用户请求，以及沿委派链携带衰减策略。

Result: 集中式层级在通用多智能体条件下达到93.9-98.6%任务完成率，优于对等网络的85.7-87.0%；但未授权操作发生率为2.7-19.8%，远高于对等网络的0.6-0.8%，且差距随层级深度扩大。重新锚定策略在所有模型配置下减少未授权操作，平均仅损失1.6个百分点完成率；链传播策略则牺牲多达36.3个百分点的必需工作完成率。

Conclusion: MasDrift揭示了集中化权衡（centralization tradeoff），使授权保留成为多智能体系统设计可量化的属性。失败源于协调机制而非模型能力本身。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) decompose long-horizon tasks across supervisors and subagents, but delegated goals do not necessarily carry their original authorization boundaries. Existing safety benchmarks mainly study adversarial compromise, while work on constraint drift lacks controlled architecture-level evaluation. We introduce MasDrift, a benchmark of 600 benign productivity tasks across eight domains. Each task pairs required work with reserved actions. MasDrift compares single-agent, centralized, and decentralized coordination while varying hierarchy depth and peer width, measuring task completion and authorization preservation. Across generic multi-agent conditions, centralized hierarchies achieve 93.9--98.6% task completion versus 85.7--87.0% for peer networks, while unauthorized actions occur in 2.7--19.8% of tasks versus 0.6--0.8%, a gap that widens with hierarchy depth. We further compare two defenses that differ in where authorization evidence resides. One re-anchors every pending call to the original user request. The other carries an attenuated policy along the delegation chain. Re-anchoring reduces unauthorized actions in every model configuration we evaluate, at a cost of 1.6 points of pooled completion. Chain propagation blocks required work instead, forfeiting up to 36.3 points. A heterogeneous case study confirms that the failure follows from coordination rather than model strength. MasDrift exposes a centralization tradeoff and makes authorization preservation a measurable property of MAS design.

</details>


### [149] [Mobility, Memory, and Network Structure in Agent-Based Models of Convention Tipping and Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07810)
*Joe Shymanski,Garrick Springer,Sandip Sen*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文通过构建一个基于主体的仿真模型，研究局部移动性、有限记忆和网络拓扑如何共同影响少数派推动群体改变既有惯例的"临界点"动态。研究发现许多配置下转变不可避免，因此重点转向预测完全采纳所需的时间，并提出了一个统一的预测模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展经典的临界点（tipping-point）研究，将结构与行为因素不仅与惯例改变的可能性联系起来，还与其发生的时间尺度联系起来。现有研究多关注"是否发生转变"，而本文关注"多久完成转变"。

Method: 使用自定义的基于代理的仿真框架（agent-based simulation），代理持有两种行为状态之一，移动的坚定少数派试图推翻现有惯例。系统性地操控局部移动性、代理有限记忆和网络拓扑三个因素，并通过统一预测模型估计完全采纳所需时间。

Result: 在许多配置下，临界点转变实际上不可避免——只要有足够时间，群体总会收敛到少数派状态。移动性是缩短收敛时间的首要加速因素，记忆和连接性则以系统性的方式调节收敛过程。

Conclusion: 本文不仅连接了结构与行为因素对惯例变更可能性的影响，还揭示了它们对变更时间尺度的影响，为规范变迁和其他类似传染性社会过程提供了通用机制参考。

Abstract: Tipping-point dynamics describe the critical conditions under which a committed minority drives a population to abandon an established convention in favor of a new one. We present a transparent agent-based model of this process, in which agents hold one of two behavioral states and a mobile committed minority attempts to overturn the incumbent convention. Our goal was to examine how localized mobility, bounded agent memory, and network topology jointly influence the tipping threshold. Using a custom agent-based simulation framework, we found that in many configurations, tipping becomes effectively inevitable: given sufficient time, the population always converges to the minority state. This observation motivated a complementary analysis focused on the pace of convergence rather than its feasibility. We introduce a unified predictive model that accurately estimates how structural and behavioral parameters determine the time required for complete adoption, showing that mobility is the dominant accelerator while memory and connectivity modulate convergence in systematic ways. Together, these results extend classical tipping-point research by linking structural and behavioral factors not only to the likelihood of convention change but also to the timescale on which it unfolds. While we frame the model in terms of convention-like binary behavioral adoption, the same mechanisms bear on norm change and other contagion-like social processes.

</details>


### [150] [Per-Shipment Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Intermodal Freight Routing Under Hurricane Disruption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07824)
*Aliza Sharmin,Xudong Wang,Mustafa Can Camur,Xueping Li*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究气候极端事件下的多式联运网络韧性路由问题，将货运路由建模为Dec-POMDP，使用Independent PPO（IPPO）在15个枢纽网络上与启发式基线对比，发现IPPO在吞吐量上最优（+12.7%），而容量感知启发式在韧性指数（RI）和延迟上领先；在需求激增场景下IPPO的RI优势扩大至+6.4%，表明学习路由在容量稀缺时最有价值。MAPPO变体因训练-评估队列不匹配而性能崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候变化导致的极端事件（如飓风）会同时使多条多式联运货运走廊退化，对货运网络构成日益增长的干扰风险，现有方法缺乏在分散执行下应对此类多走廊并发失效的智能路由策略。

Method: 将货运路由建模为带逐货件行动粒度的Dec-POMDP，采用集中训练-分散执行（CTDE）框架下的Independent PPO（IPPO）算法，在15个枢纽网络上模拟飓风干扰情景，并与两个具有特权状态访问的启发式基线进行对比。

Result: 在30个匹配回合中，无单一策略全面占优：IPPO实现最高吞吐量（+12.7%）和交付率；容量感知启发式在韧性指数（RI）和延迟上领先。在需求激增（2.9:1容量比）下，IPPO的RI优势增至+6.4%。MAPPO变体因训练-评估队列不匹配而崩溃（RI=0.811），重训练后恢复至1.018，但IPPO仍领先吞吐量。

Conclusion: 学习型路由在容量稀缺时最有价值，IPPO在吞吐量上具有优势；但集中式critic在逐货件调度场景下仍存在局限性，MAPPO对训练-评估分布不匹配敏感，需要进一步改进。

Abstract: Intermodal freight networks face growing disruption risk from climate extremes that degrade multiple corridors simultaneously. To address this, we formulate freight routing as a Dec-POMDP with per-shipment action granularity and train Independent PPO (IPPO) under Centralized Training with Decentralized Execution, comparing against two heuristic baselines with privileged state access on a 15-hub network under hurricane disruption. Across 30 matched episodes, no single policy dominates: IPPO achieves the highest throughput ($+12.7\%$) and delivery rate while a capacity-aware heuristic leads on Resilience Index (RI) and delay. Under demand surge (2.9:1 capacity ratio), IPPO's RI advantage grows to $+6.4\%$, suggesting learned routing is most valuable when capacity is scarce. A Multi-Agent PPO (MAPPO) variant collapses under train-eval queue mismatch ($\mathrm{RI} = 0.811$); retraining recovers RI to $1.018$ but IPPO still leads on throughput, pointing to residual limitations in centralized critics under per-shipment dispatch.

</details>


### [151] [Decentralized Multi-Agent Urban Traffic Management via Spatio-Temporal Mobility Profile Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08035)
*Lorenzo Mario Amorosa,Lorenzo Farina,Vittorio Todisco,Alessandro Bazzi*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出VeloCity，一个面向网联自动驾驶汽车（CAVs）的去中心化多智能体时空移动性轨迹规划框架，可在任意城市区域中实现冲突避免的车辆轨迹规划，显著降低出行时间并防止拥堵死锁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CAV交通管理方法存在局部优化、通信开销大、缺乏确定性保证、难以扩展到大型复杂城市网络等问题，需要一种可规模化且去中心化的全局轨迹规划框架。

Method: VeloCity采用去中心化架构，将移动性轨迹优化任务分配给每辆CAV。车辆向本地交通协调器查询预约表，独立计算最快的无冲突移动性轨迹，再向协调器预约其所请求的时空槽位。该框架原生适配任意道路拓扑，无需场景特定调参，同时保证无碰撞和物理可执行的车辆轨迹。

Result: 在东京、曼哈顿、罗马和博洛尼亚四个大规模真实城市地图上的仿真表明，与SOTA模型相比，VeloCity显著降低出行时间，tightly bounds delay variance（严格控制延迟方差），并在极高车辆密度下成功防止拥堵死锁。

Conclusion: VeloCity作为一种可扩展的去中心化框架，能够在任意复杂城市道路网络中实现高效且安全的CAV轨迹规划，为下一代智能交通管理提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: As modern cities face increasingly severe traffic congestion, connected and autonomous vehicles (CAVs) have emerged as a crucial enabling technology for next-generation intelligent traffic management. However, fully realizing this potential is hindered by the limitations of current paradigms. Existing approaches typically optimize localized interactions rather than system-wide efficiency, incur severe communication overhead, or lack the deterministic guarantees required for safe kinematic execution. Furthermore, current multi-agent adaptations are frequently restricted to small predefined scenarios, failing to scale across large and complex urban networks. To bridge this gap, this paper introduces VeloCity, a decentralized multi-agent spatio-temporal mobility profile planning framework designed for CAVs operating in arbitrary urban areas. To minimize vehicles' travel times, VeloCity distributes mobility profile optimization directly to individual CAVs. Vehicles query a localized traffic coordinator for a reservation table, independently compute their fastest conflict-free mobility profile, and reserve their requested space-time slots back with the coordinator. By natively adapting to any arbitrary road topology, the framework manages highly irregular urban areas without requiring scenario-specific tuning, all while guaranteeing collision-free and physically executable vehicle trajectories. Extensive simulations across four large-scale real-world urban maps (Tokyo, Manhattan, Rome, and Bologna) demonstrate the framework's scalability. Compared to established state-of-the-art models, VeloCity yields drastically lower travel times, tightly bounds delay variance, and successfully prevents congestion gridlocks even under extremely high vehicular densities.

</details>


### [152] [AOC-CBS: Anytime-Optimal Continuous-time Conflict-Based Search for Generalised Multi-Agent Path Finding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08175)
*Alvin Combrink,Sabino Francesco Roselli,Martin Fabian*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出AOC-CBS，一个面向广义MAPF（多智能体路径规划）问题的精确搜索引擎求解器，支持异构智能体、非几何冲突、任务序列等更广泛的建模假设，能够在任意时间返回带已知最优性间隙上界的可行解，并逐步收敛到最优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MAPF研究假设过于简化（离散时间、圆形智能体、共享空间图、单目标、到达目标后必须停留），无法覆盖实际应用中的异构车队、非几何冲突、任务序列等复杂场景。作者希望推广MAPF问题表述并提供一个更强大、可扩展的求解器。

Method: 作者将MAPF公式化推广以解除原有假设限制，并提出AOC-CBS（Anytime-Optimal Continuous-time Conflict-Based Search），这是一种精确且解完备的求解器。它能在运行期间始终报告具有已知最优性间隙上界的当前可行解，同时保证最终返回最优解。该求解器可配置一组修复函数（其中新引入了Tier-Prioritized Safe Interval Path Planning），并能利用多处理器核心并行计算。

Result: 在混合非凸智能体车队沿平滑运动学可行轨迹运行的场景中进行了验证。初步实验对比精确求解器OC-CBS，在知名基准和从中采样的道路上，AOC-CBS在寻找最优解方面的表现相当，同时在接受有界最优性间隙时可将可扩展性从数十个智能体提升到数百个。

Conclusion: AOC-CBS在保持最优解能力的同时显著提升了大规模场景下的扩展性，为广义MAPF问题提供了实用的 anytime 求解方案。

Abstract: Many research fields share a common structure: a set of agents, each pursuing its own goal, whose actions must be coordinated so that no two of them conflict. Multi-Agent Path Finding (MAPF) is a concrete instance of this structure, with applications from warehouses to road traffic and airports. Much of MAPF research assumes discrete time, circular agents sharing one spatial graph, a single goal per agent, and that an agent must remain at its goal once reached, precluding heterogeneous fleets, non-geometric conflicts, task sequences, and agents that move on after completing them. We generalise the MAPF formulation to lift these assumptions, and present Anytime-Optimal Continuous-time Conflict-Based Search (AOC-CBS), an exact and solution-complete solver for it. AOC-CBS guarantees the eventual return of an optimal solution, while reporting an incumbent with a known optimality gap upper bound throughout its runtime; it is configurable with a portfolio of repair functions, one of which we introduce (Tier-Prioritized Safe Interval Path Planning), and can exploit multiple processor cores. We demonstrate AOC-CBS on a mixed fleet of non-convex agents moving along smooth, kinodynamically feasible trajectories. Preliminary experiments against the exact solver OC-CBS, on well-known benchmarks and roadmaps we sample from them, show AOC-CBS is comparable at finding optimal solutions while extending scalability from the tens to the hundreds of agents when a bounded optimality gap is accepted.

</details>


### [153] [Fluid Structure, Rigid Record: A Layered Organizational Design Framework for Agent-Native Organizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08516)
*Lucian Zhu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"代理原生"的组织结构框架，将持久层和动态层分离，通过记录架构、权限/特权边界、工作流协议和三类角色组来构建可治理、可恢复的多代理系统，实现了"执行面灵活、结构面刚性"的设计目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大多数多代理系统（MAS）仅将组织化表达为对话拓扑、角色提示或固定工作流，缺乏真正的组织治理结构。作者认为代理组织不应只是带有职位的模型实例集合，需要一种结构化的、可治理的组织框架。

Method: 提出四层架构：（1）持久层：四存储记录架构与常驻专业代理池；（2）协调层：定义Permission（操作边界）和Privilege（状态变更权限）；（3）运行时层：外部工作流协议与隔离运行时存储动态组装任务组；（4）人机交互层：通过控制平面和非决策型翻译代理暴露组织。同时引入三个正交角色组（执行/审查/监督）实现权力分离。

Result: 该架构已作为原型实现并在小样本实验中评估。大规模实证验证尚未完成，因此未宣称通用性能提升，贡献在于提供了一个连贯且可证伪的代理原生组织设计、治理、恢复与评估框架。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了执行面流体化（任务和事件可改变团队组成、拓扑、视图、工具和工作流）与结构面刚性化（记录、写入约束、权限边界和权力分离保持持久）的统一，为代理组织治理提供了系统化的理论支撑。

Abstract: An agentic organization should not be a set of model instances with corporate titles, despite most MAS still operationalizing organization as a conversational topology, a role prompt, or a fixed workflow. This paper develops an agent-native organizational structure framework that separates the persistent and dynamic layers of operations. The persistent layer consists of a four-store record architecture and a pool of resident specialization agents. A coordination layer defines Permission as the boundary of the operational world available to an agent, and Privilege as the set of organizational state changes that the agent is authorized to initiate. Together, these mechanisms compile task-specific operational worlds. A runtime layer combines an external Workflow Protocol with an isolated runtime store to dynamically assemble Task Groups. A human-interaction layer exposes the organization through a Control Plane mediated by a non-decision-making Translation Agent. Three orthogonal Role Groups further separate Operation, Review, and Supervision. Operators execute within narrowly scoped leases; reviewers receive elevated but demand-activated authority to modify organizational state; supervisors retain broad observational access while holding limited modification authority. The resulting architecture is fluid at the execution surface but structurally rigid underneath: tasks and events may alter team composition, topology, views, tools, and workflows, while records, write constraints, authority boundaries, and separation of powers remain persistent. The architecture has been implemented as a prototype and evaluated in small-sample experiments. Large-scale empirical validation remains incomplete, therefore no general performance claimed is made yet. Instead, the contribution is a coherent and falsifiable framework for designing, governing, recovering, and evaluating agent-native organizations.

</details>


### [154] [Muscle Memory for Agents: Compile not Merely Retrieve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08995)
*Pouya Ghiasnezhad Omran,Soujanya Lanka,Qin Zhang,Tanya Dixit*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出"肌肉记忆"（Muscle Memory）范式：将用户重复出现的意图编译为专用智能体（specialist agents），而非像传统LLM智能体那样依赖检索式记忆。通过四阶段流水线（Harvest→Analyze→Augment→Evaluate）从对话历史中挖掘行为模式，生成质量门控的专业智能体，在90个场景中实现88.9%的胜率和+2.05的个性化增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体记忆架构（文本/嵌入/反思/规则+检索+通用编排）在个性化场景中并非最优选择，用户需要反复纠正格式、深度和范围（多轮税），本文主张编译（compilation）比检索（retrieval）更适合个性化工作负载。

Method: 提出四阶段流水线：Harvest（挖掘对话历史）→ Analyze（分离行为模式与任务模式）→ Augment（增强）→ Evaluate（质量门控），生成可执行的专业编译智能体，并采用两阶段触发匹配机制。

Result: 在90个保留场景（5个用户画像）中，增强后的助手在36个专业智能体触发场景中赢得32个，胜率88.9%，个性化增益+2.05，准确率仅损失0.28（1-4分制）。

Conclusion: 编译范式在个性化场景中优于检索范式，该结果对更广泛的记忆设计空间有启示意义，但仍存在开放问题待解决。

Abstract: Memory for LLM agents has converged on a single architectural pattern: store experience as text, embeddings, reflections, or rules; retrieve at inference time; let a general-purpose orchestrator interpret what to do. This paper argues that the pattern is the wrong default for personalization. We position Muscle Memory - the practice of compiling recurring user intent into purpose-built specialist agents - as a distinct memory paradigm from retrieval, and we argue that compilation is a better fit for the workloads where current assistants impose a multi-turn tax on their users: making them repeatedly correct format, depth, and scope to obtain a domain-appropriate answer. We support the position with a reference implementation and empirical evidence. The implementation is a four-phase pipeline (Harvest $\rightarrow$ Analyze $\rightarrow$ Augment $\rightarrow$ Evaluate) that mines conversational history, separates behavioral from task patterns, and emits quality-gated executable compiled specialists with two-stage trigger matching. On 90 held-out scenarios across five user personas, the augmented assistant wins 32 of 36 cases where a specialist fires, an 88.9% win rate, with a +2.05 personalization gain and only a $-0.28$ accuracy cost on a 1-4 scale. We discuss why compilation is better suited than retrieval in this regime, what the result implies for the broader memory design space, and what open problems remain.

</details>


### [155] [Beyond Tier Labels: Role- and Deployment-Dependent Model Substitution in Multi-Call LLM Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09155)
*Renxiang Wang,Jiaming Cui*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种谓词-动作分解方法，将大语言模型多调用系统中的"在哪里使用强模型"与"替换模型带来的收益"两个决策分离，通过solve-merge-verify工作流实验证明模型替换的价值取决于部署上下文而非模型层级标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的查询级路由方法无法捕捉大型多调用LLM系统的价值：模型的价值取决于它在依赖计算中的位置以及周围的部署环境。传统路由器只决定在哪里使用更强的模型，却将替换本身的收益视为已知。

Method: 通过谓词-动作分解（predicate-action factorization）将路由决策分离为"位置选择"和"替换动作"两个独立部分，在8-64次求解调用的solve-merge-verify工作流中，使用四个三层模型阶梯进行受控实验评估。

Result: 在数值频率计数任务中，全强模型组合将RMSE从4.818降至1.538（Mixed Qwen/GPT阶梯），而平均Qwen-only排序结果相反；输入匹配干预显示相同的medium-to-strong动作在不同角色和规模下价值差异显著；语义任务和契约的转变再次反转了排序；分配消融区分了有用的稀疏放置与欠覆盖和盲目升级。

Conclusion: 模型替换应被视为一种依赖于部署条件的动作，而非由模型层级标签隐含的属性。大规模工作流路由应遵循：校准动作、解析角色条件效应、优化放置位置。

Abstract: Large multi-call LLM systems pose a scientific problem that query-level routing does not capture: the value of a model depends on where it enters a dependent computation and on the deployment that surrounds that call. Existing routers typically decide \emph{where} to spend a stronger model while treating the benefit of the substitution itself as known. We separate these two decisions through a predicate-action factorization and evaluate it in controlled solve-merge-verify workflows spanning 8-64 solve calls and four three-tier model ladders. The resulting evidence reveals a consistent principle beneath apparently conflicting outcomes. On numeric frequency counting, all-strong reduces RMSE from 4.818 to 1.538 in the Mixed Qwen/GPT ladder, whereas the average Qwen-only ordering reverses. Input-matched interventions further show that the same medium-to-strong action has sharply different value across roles and scales. A semantic task-and-contract shift reverses the Mixed ordering again, while allocation ablations distinguish useful sparse placement from under-coverage and indiscriminate escalation. Together, these results establish model substitution as a deployment-conditioned action rather than a property implied by a tier label, and they provide a practical sequence for large-scale workflow routing: calibrate the action, resolve its role-conditioned effect, and then optimize its placement.

</details>


### [156] [MoRSE: Task-Oriented Multi-Agent System with Mixture of Role-Subtask Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09251)
*Peiwen Li,Shiyang Zhang,Yangtian Zhang,Sizhuang He,David van Dijk,Rex Ying*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出MoRSE，一种面向任务的多智能体系统，通过（角色、子任务）条件化专业化在任务结构和参数两个层面区分智能体，并采用原型语义路由器和层次化组相对策略优化，在代码生成基准上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的多智能体系统主要依赖粗粒度的提示词级别区分，缺乏参数层面的适应性调整，导致智能体间异质性不足、专业化能力有限，成为复杂长时程任务的性能瓶颈。

Method: MoRSE包含三个核心组件：(1)任务结构层面，将任务分解为依赖感知的有向无环图(DAG)，为每个智能体分配特定的(角色, 子任务)；(2)参数层面，提出动态的(角色, 子任务)LoRA专家混合模块，配合基于原型的语义路由器；(3)优化层面，提出分层组相对策略优化，通过两层信用分配隔离专家更新与路由决策带来的方差，解耦专家质量与路由质量。

Result: 在三个主干模型上的代码生成基准实验中，MoRSE在整体任务和逐步（step-wise）性能上均取得提升，且训练获得的专业化能力可泛化到未见的任务类别和领域。

Conclusion: 通过任务结构和参数层面的双重专业化，MoRSE有效增强了多智能体系统的异质性和专业化能力，验证了在复杂任务上的有效性及跨领域的泛化性。

Abstract: Large language model-based multi-agent systems have recently shown strong potential for complex, long-horizon tasks. However, existing methods mainly rely on coarse prompt-level differentiation without parameter adaptation for diverse subtasks, resulting in insufficient inter-agent heterogeneity and limited specialized capability that bottleneck performance on tasks with complex requirements. To address this, we introduce a Task-Oriented Multi-Agent System with Mixture of Role-Subtask Experts (MoRSE) that distinguishes agents with (role, subtask)-conditional specialization at both the task structure and parameter levels. To make agents' responsibility explicit at the task structure level, we formulate a task-oriented multi-agent system that decomposes each task into a dependency-aware Directed Acyclic Graph of subtasks and assigns each agent a specific (role, subtask), introducing task-level specialization across collaborating agents. Additionally, to address the diverse role and subtask parameter adaptation demands, we propose a dynamic Mixture of (role, subtask) LoRA Experts module with a prototype-based semantic router for subtasks, augmenting agents with parameter-level specialization on a shared LLM substrate cost-effectively. Then, to co-optimize experts and router stably under sparse task rewards, we further propose a hierarchical group-relative policy optimization with two-layer credit assignment that isolates expert updates from the cross-route variance introduced by routing decisions, disentangling expert quality from routing quality. Experiments on code-generation benchmarks across three backbones demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach, with improvements in both whole-task and step-wise performance, and the gains from trained specialization generalize across held-out task categories and domains.

</details>


### [157] [Distributed Team Orchestration via Supervisor Networks: Convergence, Optimality, and Resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09256)
*Juntian Zhu,Guanpu Chen,Tongtian Zhu,Miguel de Carvalho,Zhouwang Yang,Fengxiang He*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有监督网络的零和势团队博弈，提出分布式团队编排算法（DTOA），结合团队虚构博弈与基于监督者的分布式信念学习，证明信念估计收敛且学习动力学收敛到近似团队纳什均衡；在拜占庭团队误报场景下开发了具有弹性的DTOA，并提供拜占庭团队识别的概率保证和渐近界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在包含监督网络的零和势团队博弈中，智能体依赖监督者提供的信念信息而非准确公共信念，但该信息可能因监督者的信念估计误差和拜占庭团队对联合行动的误报而不准确，需要设计能够处理这些不准确性的分布式学习算法。

Method: 提出分布式团队编排算法（DTOA），该算法结合了团队虚构博弈与基于监督者的分布式信念学习。在拜占庭设置下，考虑误报攻击模型，开发了拜占庭弹性的DTOA变体。

Result: 证明了监督者信念估计的收敛性，并建立了诱导学习动力学在团队纳什差距（TNG）意义上收敛到近似团队纳什均衡（TNE）的结果。在拜占庭场景下提供了拜占庭团队识别的概率保证，并建立了诚实TNG的渐近界。数值实验验证了理论发现并与基线方法进行了比较。

Conclusion: DTOA算法能够有效处理监督者信念不准确问题，在正常和拜占庭攻击场景下均能收敛到近似团队纳什均衡，并在马尔可夫决策过程设置中表现出良好的性能。

Abstract: In this paper, we study zero-sum potential team games with a supervisor network, where agents rely on supervisor-provided belief information rather than accurate common beliefs. The main challenge is that such belief information can be inaccurate because of supervisors' belief-estimation errors and the misreporting of joint actions by Byzantine teams. We propose the distributed team-orchestrating algorithm (DTOA), which combines team fictitious play with supervisor-based distributed belief learning. We prove the convergence of supervisors' belief estimates and establish that the induced learning dynamics converge to a near team-Nash equilibrium (TNE) in terms of the team-Nash gap (TNG). In the Byzantine setting, we consider a misreporting attack model and develop a Byzantine-resilient DTOA. We further provide probabilistic guarantees for Byzantine-team identification and establish an asymptotic bound on the honest TNG. Numerical experiments illustrate the theoretical findings, compare DTOA with baseline learning methods, and evaluate its performance in a Markov decision process setting.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [158] [Application of Artificial Intelligence for Fraudulent Banking Operations Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07471)
*Bohdan Mytnyk,Oleksandr Tkachyk,Nataliya Shakhovska,Solomiia Fedushko,Yuriy Syerov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究利用机器学习算法识别银行欺诈交易，重点探讨了处理不平衡数据集、特征转换和特征工程等技术，提出基于人工神经网络的模型，并对比多种算法，最终堆叠泛化获得最佳AUC约0.954。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于COVID-19疫情导致大量银行业务转移至线上平台，银行欺诈案件显著增加，需要有效的智能识别方法来保障用户资金安全。

Method: 采用多种机器学习算法进行欺诈交易识别，包括人工神经网络和逻辑回归等，并应用数据预处理技术处理高度不平衡数据集、特征转换和特征工程以提升检测精度，同时使用堆叠泛化方法提升性能。

Result: 逻辑回归算法表现最佳，AUC约为0.946；堆叠泛化方法进一步提升至AUC 0.954。

Conclusion: 利用人工智能算法识别银行欺诈是当前数字化社会中具有重要现实意义的研究方向，堆叠泛化方法能有效提高欺诈检测的准确性。

Abstract: This study considers the task of applying artificial intelligence to recognize bank fraud. In recent years, due to the COVID19 pandemic, bank fraud has become even more common due to the massive transition of many operations to online platforms and the creation of many charitable funds that criminals can use to deceive users. The present work focuses on machine learning algorithms as a tool well suited for analyzing and recognizing online banking transactions. The study`s scientific novelty is the development of machine learning models for identifying fraudulent banking transactions and techniques for preprocessing bank data for further comparison and selection of the best results. This paper also details various methods for improving detection accuracy, i.e., handling highly imbalanced datasets, feature transformation, and feature engineering. The proposed model, which is based on an artificial neural network, effectively improves the accuracy of fraudulent transaction detection. The results of the different algorithms are visualized, and the logistic regression algorithm performs the best, with an output AUC value of approximately 0,946. The stacked generalization shows a better AUC of 0.954. The recognition of banking fraud using artificial intelligence algorithms is a topical issue in our digital society.

</details>


### [159] [Data-Driven Fire-Zone Segmentation for Improved Short-Term Wildfire Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07472)
*Nicolas Caron,Christophe Guyeux,Hassan Noura,Benjamin Aynes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于分水岭检测与K-means聚类相结合的无监督火灾区域分割算法，取代传统的均匀网格离散化方法，用于短期野火预测。实验表明该方法在法国六个省份和六个预测模型上均优于网格方法，平均IoU提升3-6%，且计算开销极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统野火预测模型将研究区域划分为均匀网格，忽略了点火事件在空间分布上的异质性。作者认为数据离散化方式对预测性能的影响比模型选择更大，因此提出挑战这一范式。

Method: 提出一种无监督火灾区域分割算法，结合分水岭检测与K-means聚类，直接基于历史火灾模式定义预测单元，取代均匀网格离散化。

Result: 在法国六个省份和六个预测模型上的实验表明，火灾区域分割方法一致优于基于网格的方法，平均IoU提升3-6%（取决于空间尺度）。该方法计算轻量（每个配置<10秒）且完全可并行化。

Conclusion: 优化空间离散化策略能够为短期野火预测带来显著且可复现的性能提升，数据离散化方式比模型选择更为关键。

Abstract: Wildfire prediction models typically discretize study areas into uniform grids, ignoring the heterogeneous spatial distribution of ignitions. We challenge this paradigm by showing that how data is discretized matters more than which model is used. We propose an unsupervised fire-zone segmentation algorithm combining watershed detection with K-means clustering to define prediction units directly from historical fire patterns. Experiments across six French departments and six forecasting models show that fire-zone segmentation consistently outperforms grid-based approaches, with mean IoU improvements of +3--6% depending on spatial scale. The method is computationally lightweight (<10s per configuration) and fully parallelizable. Our results demonstrate that optimizing spatial discretization yields significant, reproducible performance gains for short-term wildfire forecasting.

</details>


### [160] [Evolving Safety Landscape of Multi-modal Large Language Models: A Survey of Emerging Threats and Safeguards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07535)
*Xi Li,Shu Zhao,Xiaohan Zou,Fei Zhao,Fuxiao Liu,Yusen Zhang,Cheng Han,Yushun Dong,Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理了多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的安全格局演变，提出了基于多模态的安全威胁分类法，分析了威胁模型的转变，并总结了最新的安全策略及未来挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLMs通过模态对齐与融合增强了理解与推理能力，但其架构复杂性带来了新的安全威胁，如模态集成受损、模态错配和融合安全风险。现有基于单模态假设的安全框架无法充分应对这些新挑战，亟需系统性分析。

Method: 本文为综述性研究：首先提出多模态接地（multimodal grounded）的安全威胁分类法，分析威胁模型转变，覆盖对抗攻击、数据投毒、越狱和幻觉；随后总结更新后的安全假设，并据此组织归纳近期MLLM安全策略；最后讨论开放性挑战与未来研究方向。

Result: 提出了针对MLLMs的新型安全威胁分类体系，梳理了威胁模型从单模态到多模态的转变，并系统整理了适应多模态场景的最新安全防御策略。

Conclusion: MLLMs的安全问题需要超越传统单模态框架，构建更原则性且可扩展的多模态安全机制，未来的安全研究应关注多模态特有的威胁特征与防御方案。

Abstract: Multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) integrate heterogeneous modalities through modality alignment and fusion, enabling stronger understanding and reasoning. However, this architectural shift reshapes the safety landscape of machine learning. Increased model complexity and cross-modal interactions give rise to novel threats, including compromised modality integration, modality misalignment, and fused safety risks, reflecting shifts in threat modeling beyond uni-modal assumptions. These shifts, in turn, impose new constraints on safety solutions not captured by existing frameworks rooted in uni-modal learning. Motivated by these challenges, this survey provides a systematic analysis of the evolving safety landscape of MLLMs. We first propose a multimodal grounded taxonomy of safety threats and analyze shifts in threat models, covering adversarial attacks, data poisoning, jailbreaks, and hallucinations. We then summarize updated safety assumptions and organize recent advances in MLLM safety strategies accordingly. Finally, we discuss open challenges and future directions to inform the development of more principled and scalable safety mechanisms for multimodal systems.

</details>


### [161] [Tracing sources of epistemic uncertainty in deep learning predictions: homo- and hetero-scedastic linearized estimators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07630)
*Pierre Nodet,Thomas George*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过将两种经典统计估计器适配到现代深度学习，利用近似Fisher信息矩阵实现了对认知不确定性和偶然不确定性的量化，并在实际架构上验证了其实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习中不确定性量化对模型鲁棒性至关重要，但现有方法难以在大规模架构上区分两种不同来源的不确定性（偶然不确定性和数据稀缺导致的不确定性），需要可扩展到实际架构的有效方案。

Method: 将两种经典统计估计器适配到深度学习框架，利用近期在近似Fisher信息矩阵方面的进展，实现对两种不确定性来源的分解与量化，并扩展到实际大规模架构。

Result: 实验表明每个测试点受两种不确定性来源的影响程度不同，证明了所提估计器在提高真实世界应用鲁棒性方面的实际效用。

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效区分偶然不确定性与数据稀缺性导致的不确定性，为深度学习的不确定性量化提供了可扩展且实用的工具。

Abstract: We adapt two classical statistical estimators for quantifying uncertainty to modern deep learning, in order to provide clearer insights into uncertainty attributable to two sources : aleatoric uncertainty, or locally scarce data. Our approach leverages recent advances in approximate Fisher Information Matrices, to enable scaling to actual architectures. Experimental results demonstrate how each test points is differentially impacted by both sources, highlighting the practical utility of our estimators in improving the robustness of real-world applications.

</details>


### [162] [SkillConsist: Detecting Inconsistencies in Agent Skills via Bidirectional Graph Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07639)
*Chaofan Meng,Yuhang Zheng,Yingnan Zhou,Sihan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillConsist方法，通过双向图对齐和图文差异分析来检测LLM Agent技能中声明与实现行为之间的一致性，在633个技能基准上实现了87.93%的F1分数，优于最佳基线20.43个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent Skill（智能体技能）提供LLM代理可复用的能力，但技能声明与实现行为之间的不一致可能暴露未披露的危险行为或导致错误的技能选择。现有方法依赖预定义类别或声明范围来评估行为一致性，难以处理声明和实现内容在文本与代码中的混合分布，以及一条简洁声明可能对应多个关联实现步骤的问题。

Method: SkillConsist包含四个关键步骤：1) 利用LLM将声明和实现内容分别分离为行为记录，并通过静态分析补充实现记录；2) 分别构建声明侧和实现侧的行为图；3) 从任意一侧的行为记录出发，进行双向图对齐，在另一侧搜索候选子图并按行为关系扩展直至完整表达源侧行为；4) 通过图差异分析识别对齐子图之间的冲突并输出检测结果。

Result: 在从ClawHub最热门的500个公开技能和133个技能注入包构建的633个技能基准上，SkillConsist在包级检测上达到86.85%的精确率、89.03%的召回率和87.93%的F1分数，比最佳基线提高了20.43个百分点；在局部化定位上达到67.60%精确率、58.14%召回率和62.52%的F1分数。

Conclusion: SkillConsist通过行为图构建和双向图对齐有效解决了Agent Skill声明与实现行为一致性检测问题，在图谱层面实现了精确的一致性对比，显著超越了现有基线方法。

Abstract: Agent Skills provide reusable capabilities to LLM agents. Agent Skill inconsistencies can expose undisclosed dangerous behavior or cause wrong Skill selection. Recent Agent Skill research has increasingly examined Agent Skill consistency detection. Existing methods evaluate behaviors or security-property graphs against predefined categories or declared scopes. More recently, PL-HCL uses an LLM-based model to learn consistency across metadata, instructions, and resources. However, declaration and implementation behavior can be mixed across text and code, and a concise declaration can correspond to multiple connected implementation steps. We present SkillConsist to address both challenges. An LLM separates declaration and implementation content into behavior records on the implementation and declaration sides, while static analysis supplements implementation records. These records form declaration and implementation behavior graphs, respectively. Starting from a behavior record on either side, bidirectional graph alignment searches the other graph for a candidate subgraph and expands it along behavior relations until it completely expresses the source-side behavior. Graph differencing identifies conflicts between aligned subgraphs and outputs the detection results. We construct a 633-Skill benchmark from ClawHub's 500 most-downloaded public Skills and 133 Skill-Inject packages. The benchmark contains 319 inconsistent and 314 consistent Skills and 442 localized inconsistency annotations. On this benchmark, SkillConsist achieves 86.85% precision, 89.03% recall, and 87.93% F1 for package-level detection, improving F1 over the best baseline by 20.43 percentage points. For localization, it achieves 67.60% precision, 58.14% recall, and 62.52% F1.

</details>


### [163] [PhysAttNet: Enhancing Predictive Performance in Industrial and Astrophysical Time Series via Physics-Informed Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07681)
*Amal Saadallah,Julia Tjus,Petra Wiederkeher,Wolfgang Rhode*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PhysAttNet，一个物理信息引导的注意力框架，用于时间序列预测。它通过在轻量级CNN预测器上添加受领域知识正则化约束的注意力头，引入三项可微正则化（对齐、平滑、稀疏），在铣削切削力预测和耀变体耀斑预测两个应用中提升了预测精度、泛化能力和重要事件预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理过程时间序列预测需要应对噪声、变异和测量不确定性，CNNs虽计算高效但学到的时序表征常呈现不稳定或物理不一致的注意力模式，降低鲁棒性、泛化性和可解释性，因此需要引入物理引导的归纳偏置。
